⏺ Ticket 18, the set-piece model, is created and claimed. Ticket 12 now waits on
  it, and tactic familiarity is out of scope.

  One correction to what I told you: the set-pieces decision request wasn't
  open. It was answered on 2026-09-19: set pieces ship as fields on the Tactic
  (note (.agents/notes/proposed/feature/2026-09-19-set-pieces-ship-as-a-tactic-f
  ield.md)). It left one question open, whether the engine uses a nomination,
  and ticket 18 answers it.

  Round 9, ticket 18:

  ❓ Q1 - What lives where:
  - In the Tactic Template: team set-piece instructions, stored per side as in
    CM:
    - Corners: short, near post, far post, edge of area, edge of six-yard box.
    - Free kicks: short, long, cross near, far or centre, aim for best header.
    - Throw-ins: short, long, quick.
  - Also in the template: each slot's set-piece roles for attacking and
    defending.
  - On the live Tactic only: the priorities, which name players. These are the
    captain and the penalty, free-kick (L/R), corner (L/R) and throw-in (L/R)
    takers, each an ordered list. This extends the 2026-09-19 note, which
    already put takers on the Tactic.

  ➡️ Accept. It matches CM's own split: tactic files held the instructions and
  roles but no players.

  ---

  ❓ Q2 - Values we haven't got yet: The research listed the team set-piece
  values but not the player set-piece role values, and didn't break down the
  priorities panels. Should a ticket 19 (AFK task, like 14) transcribe
  player_sp_instructions.xml, team_sp_instructions.xml and the priorities in
  tactics.xml? If it turns up anything that doesn't map cleanly, I'd bring it
  back to you as with ticket 14.

  ➡️ Yes.

  ---

  ❓ Q3 - How set pieces arise in the engine: Should the engine use nominations,
  answering the note's open question? Proposed mechanics:
  - Events:
    - Corner: from saved or blocked shots and cleared crosses.
    - Free Kick: from fouls, and becomes a chance in the attacking third.
    - Penalty: from fouls on attacking-phase players at a small share.
    - Long throw-ins: become a cross-like chance in the attacking third. Other
      throw-ins produce no event.
  - Delivery: the nominated taker, scaled by his crossing (corners and crossed
    free kicks) or shooting and composure (direct free kicks and penalties), the
    closest attributes available.
  - Instructions: team set-piece instructions choose the chance type, like Cross
    Aim.
  - Player roles: attacking roles choose the target; defending roles weigh the
    marking.

  ➡️ Yes. The takers matter, and it all goes through the chance pipeline from
  ticket 08.

  ---

  ❓ Q4 - Fallbacks and the captain:
  - If a nominee isn't on the pitch, the next one in the list takes it; if
    nobody listed is, the on-pitch player with the best relevant attribute does.
  - The captain's armband passes down the order the same way, as SI's 4.1.3
    patch fixed.
  - The captain shows on the team sheet and in commentary, with no effect on
    morale or the match.

  ➡️ Accept.

❯ approve all

  background.

› Message from a7eb9d2aee9243ed9
  I've added an `## Answer` section to ticket 19 (`/Users/joao/dev/audit/.scratc
  h/formations-and-instructions/issues/19-transcribe-cm-set-piece-screens.md`).
  `Status: claimed` is unchanged and verify-md-links passes. I reused the files
  already extracted under `/tmp/cm0304/x`. No git operations, and nothing from
  the game was copied into the repo.

  **Defaults.** On both instruction screens every row is a checkbox plus a
  dropdown showing "(default)". Unticked, the AI decides. Ticking it selects the
  first value in the list.

  **Team Set Piece Instructions**, all per side (Left and Right):

  | Setting | Values |
  |---|---|
  | Corners L/R | Short, Near Post, Far Post, Edge Of Area, Edge Of Six Yard Box
  |
  | Free Kicks L/R | Short, Long, Cross Near, Cross Far, Cross Centre, Aim For
  Best Header |
  | Throw Ins L/R | Short, Long, Quick |

  **Player Set Piece Instructions**, set per player:

  | Setting | Values | Per side |
  |---|---|---|
  | Defend Free Kick | 6 (Back … Far Post) | no |
  | Attack Free Kick | 7 (Always Stay Back … Run Over Ball) | no |
  | Defend Corner | 8 (Back … Close Down) | no |
  | Attack Corner | 11 (Always Stay Back … Offer Short Option) | no |
  | Attacking Throw Ins (L)/(R) | 5 (Stay Back, Come Short, Lurk Outside Area,
  Near Post, Go Forward) | yes |

  **Set Priorities** has 8 ordered lists: Captain, Penalty, and Free Kick,
  Corner and Throw In takers split Left/Right. None has a fixed length, and each
  player can appear once per list. The XML doesn't give a length, so I read it
  from `cm0304.exe`. Each list is stored with a 16-bit count, and I found no cap
  anywhere. At the match the game takes the first nominee who is on the pitch.
  A nominee outside the first eleven only triggers a warning. If you pick no
  captain, the game picks one for you.

  **Where it doesn't map onto the note's model:**
  1. Every CM setting has a "(default)" state, and the note's value lists don't
  include one.
  2. The player roles aren't a plain attacking/defending pair. Free kicks and
  corners have attack and defend roles with no side. Throw-ins have attacking
  roles only, per side. There is no penalty role.
  3. The engine rule only turns long throws into events. That leaves the Short
  and Quick throw-in settings, 4 of the 5 player throw-in roles, and most
  throw-in taker choices with no effect. The note rejects settings like that.
  4. The CM screen sets roles per player; the note puts them on template slots.
  Only the `.tac` bytes support per-slot storage.
  5. Nothing I found confirms the note's fallback to "best relevant attribute"
  for takers.
  6. Minor: the game shows the "Throw In Taker(R)" heading on both throw-in
  lists.

⏺ Agent "Transcribe CM set-piece screens" finished · 3m 40s

⏺ Ticket 19 is resolved; it finished recording CM's set-piece screens. The
  screens don't match the set-piece model you approved in ticket 18 in five
  places, so I'm bringing those back before amending it.

  What CM actually has:
  - Every set-piece setting has a "(default)" state, where the AI decides.
  - Player roles: Attack Free Kick (7 options), Defend Free Kick (6), Attack
    Corner (11), Defend Corner (8), and Attacking Throw-Ins, per side (5: stay
    back, come short, lurk outside area, near post, go forward). There is no
    penalty role.
  - Priorities: eight ordered lists with no length cap. At a set piece the game
    takes the first nominee who is on the pitch, and it picks a captain if you
    don't.

  ❓ Q1 - "(default)" values: Should every team set-piece instruction and player
  role get a default value, meaning the engine decides, as the first value and
  the default? This matches the team and player instructions.

  ➡️ Yes.

  ---

  ❓ Q2 - The role structure: Should the "attacking and defending roles per
  slot" in ticket 18 be replaced with CM's actual five settings: attack and
  defend for free kicks, attack and defend for corners, and attacking throw-ins
  per side?

  ➡️ Yes, as CM had them.

  ---

  ❓ Q3 - Throw-ins would do almost nothing: Ticket 18 only turns long throws
  into events. That leaves short and quick throws, four of the five throw-in
  roles, and most throw-in taker choices with no effect, which the effort ruled
  out. Should throw-ins in the attacking third resolve without a visible event,
  like this?
  - Short: keeping possession ↑.
  - Quick: the chance of a quick attack ↑, keeping possession ↓.
  - Long: a cross-like chance, with the taker's strength as the closest
    attribute for throw distance.
  - Roles: come short makes short throws keep the ball more often; near post and
    go forward become targets for long throws; lurk outside area gets
    knock-downs as long-shot chances; stay back reduces counter-attack risk.

  ➡️ Yes. Every throw-in setting then changes something real, still without
  simulating every throw.

  ---

  ❓ Q4 - Per slot or per player: CM's screen sets these roles per player, but
  the tactic file stores them per slot, the same as player instructions. Keep
  them per slot?

  ➡️ Yes, per slot, consistent with ticket 04. Templates hold no players.

  ---

  ❓ Q5 - Fallback for takers: The "best relevant attribute" fallback isn't
  CM's. CM takes the first nominee on the pitch and only warns about nominees
  who aren't. Should we keep our fallback for when no nominee is on the pitch,
  labelled as this game's rule, plus CM's automatic captain when none is set?

  ➡️ Yes. Ours only applies where CM's behaviour is unknown.

  Still waiting on your answer: the positional spec's three test seams from my
  previous message.

⏺ Before drafting slices, I need to know how far the old per-player position
  rows reach. That decides whether this is a wide refactor needing
  expand-then-contract.

  Listed 1 directory (ctrl+o to expand)

⏺ I've read the cm-to-tickets instructions and measured the reach of the old
  per-player position rows: six main-process files read them, plus about 25
  files that read Familiarity. Changing the storage in one step would break all
  of them at once. So I'm proposing expand-then-contract:
  - The new storage lands with a derived projection of today's (Position, tier)
    list, so every current reader keeps working unchanged.
  - Readers then move to the new model one slice at a time.
  - A final ticket deletes the projection.

  Numbering continues after the eleven decision tickets.

  Proposed breakdown

  12. The positional rules in the shared package. Blocked by: none.
      - A prefactor with no behaviour change: Line, Side and Free Role Rating
        types; suitability; derived tiers; the compact label; Position Weights
        by row and width, including the four new tables; the Slot type and phase
        by row; and the transitional mapping from Position to cell.
      - Fully unit-tested (seam 1), including the label's worked-examples table.
  13. Players are stored and generated with line, side and Free Role ratings.
      Blocked by: 12.
      - The twelve-rating player row replaces player_positions.
      - Generation uses the archetypes and the new squad demand.
      - A derived projection gives every current reader its old (Position, tier)
        list, so the whole app stays green.
      - An older save is refused with the schema-mismatch error.
      - The glossary gains Line, Side and Free Role Rating.
      - Tests: seam 2 (a seeded save).
  14. Squad, search, transfer and profile screens show compact labels and "can
      play" filters. Blocked by: 13.
      - Contracts carry the label; none carries a raw rating.
      - The position column sorts by pitch order and groups by best natural
        line.
  15. The Tactics screen and overview show suitability. Blocked by: 13.
      - Each slot shows the occupant's suitability as the fit indicator, via the
        transitional mapping.
      - The overview's tier counts come from suitability.
  16. Best XI, Overall Rating and AI squad needs read suitability directly.
      Blocked by: 13.
      - Best XI fills slots by suitability-adjusted Position Rating.
      - Overall Rating is the best Natural cell.
      - Transfers and AI judge squad needs by line and side coverage.
  17. Retraining a player toward a new line or side. Blocked by: 13.
      - The manager sets or clears a target on the player screen.
      - The command fails visibly for a player not in the manager's club, or for
        a target that isn't a line or side.
      - The weekly training tick raises that rating only.
      - Tests: seam 3.
  18. Contract offers and PlayerSigned drop the positional designation. Blocked
      by: none.
      - The offer screen, the signing command and the event carry no Role or
        Position.
  19. Contract: delete the legacy projection and reconcile the docs. Blocked by:
      14, 15, 16, 17.
      - Removes the old (Position, tier) projection and stored-Familiarity code.
      - Replaces the Position and Familiarity Tier glossary entries.
      - Moves this effort's Agent Notes from proposed/ to implemented/.
      - The Position type itself stays, because the Tactic still uses it until
        the formations spec replaces it.

  Match phase needs no ticket here. Phase by row gives exactly the same result
  as today's phase table for the ten Positions, so the engine's switch happens
  in the formations spec.

› Message from afdce61642e646268
  I found no hard code-standard violations. There is one prose-rule violation
  and two items the ticket plan defers on purpose. The rest are judgement calls.

  **Hard violation**
  - `packages/shared/src/rules/slots.ts`, the SLOT_WEIGHTS doc comment: "The
  four new ones — SW, DM wide (the wing-back), AM wide and F wide — are design
  values". `docs/agents/unslop.md` rule 13 says to avoid em dashes entirely, and
  parentheses too. Suggest: "The four new tables are design values, not
  research findings: SW, DM wide, AM wide and F wide." The commit subject has an
  em dash as well.

  **Deferred by plan (worth a line in the commit or ticket)**
  - The five linked Agent Notes are still under `.agents/notes/proposed/`.
  `packages/AGENTS.md` (Gates) says notes are "promoted to `implemented/` in the
  same commit that ships it". Ticket 19 deliberately defers this.
  - Vocabulary: `CONTEXT.md` Familiarity Tier says "_Avoid_: Proficiency,
  suitability", yet this commit exports `suitability`, `NATURAL_SUITABILITY` and
  `COMPETENT_SUITABILITY` as domain names. Slot, Line Rating, Side Rating and
  Free Role Rating are also missing from the glossary. That goes against
  `packages/AGENTS.md` (Domain vocabulary): use CONTEXT.md terms and "flag
  rather than override". Ticket 19 plans the glossary fix, but until it lands,
  CONTEXT.md contradicts the code.

  **Smells (judgement)**
  - **Duplicated Code / Repeated Switches.** Three places decide whether a
  column is L or R:
    - `widthOf` in slots.ts
    - `const isWide = slot.column === "L" || slot.column === "R";` in `lineFor`
    - `slot.column === "L" ? "L" : slot.column === "R" ? "R" : "C"` in `sideFor`

    Use `widthOf(slot.column) === "wide"` in `lineFor`, and add a
  `sideOf(column): Side` next to `widthOf`.
  - **Duplicated Code, "one home per meaning" (`packages/AGENTS.md` Roles).**
  slots.ts adds `type Phase = "defense" | "midfield" | "attack"`.
  `game-engine/src/match/tactical-modifiers.ts` already has `type Phase = keyof
  typeof PHASE_POSITIONS`, giving two Phase types for one meaning. Derive the
  shared one as `keyof typeof PHASE_POSITIONS`, or have the engine import it.
  - **Test duplicates the source it checks.** In
  `test/rules/suitability.test.ts`, "puts each Position in the phase it fed
  before" hard-codes a `before` table. The ticket asks for agreement with
  "today's phase table", so derive `before` from `PHASE_POSITIONS`. That way the
  test fails if the two drift apart. The same file also tests all of slots.ts,
  which would fit better in a `slots.test.ts`.
  - **Mysterious Name.** `overallRatingAt(attributes, ratings)` takes no slot,
  but the `-At` suffix implies one, as it does in `positionRatingAt(…, slot)`
  and `familiarityAt(…, slot)`. Suggest `overallRatingOverCells` or
  `overallRatingFromRatings`. `compactLabel`, re-exported through the index
  barrel, loses its subject; `compactPositionLabel` matches the file. The local
  `has` in `compactLabel` would read better as `qualifies`.
  - **Minor coupling.** `positionLabel.ts` imports `COMPETENT_SUITABILITY` as
  its threshold. Both happen to be 15, but the note treats the label threshold
  as CM Scout's own rule. A separate `LABEL_THRESHOLD = 15` would stop a tier
  retune from silently changing labels.

  Files: `/Users/joao/dev/audit/packages/shared/src/rules/{slots,suitability,pos
  itionLabel,positionalRatings}.ts`,
  `/Users/joao/dev/audit/packages/shared/test/rules/suitability.test.ts`.

› Message from a4a7084d7fffbe826
  Spec review of 8b2692a8 against ticket 12, the spec and the four linked notes.
  The core rules match the spec exactly: the suitability special cases, the
  tier thresholds (18 and 15), the label suppression rules, the F/S rule, GK
  short-circuit, no WB, R-L-C side order, 31 cells, 12 tables and the
  transitional mapping. I found no blocking defects. The findings below are gaps
  in the tests and small liberties.

  **(a) Missing or partial**
  - None of substance. The label test covers every example the ticket names. SW,
  DM wide, AM wide and F wide are marked as design values in the `SLOT_WEIGHTS`
  comment.
  - One partial: "Phase by row agrees with today's phase table for all ten
  mapped Positions." The test in
  `packages/shared/test/rules/suitability.test.ts` checks against a hard-coded
  `before` map, not the existing `PHASE_POSITIONS` in
  `packages/shared/src/rules/positions.ts`. The values match today, but if
  `PHASE_POSITIONS` changed, the test would stay green.

  **(b) Scope creep (all minor)**
  - `overallRatingAt` adds a fallback to "his most suitable cells" when a player
  is natural nowhere. The spec only says "The best Position Rating among cells
  where the player is Natural". The old `overallRating` falls back to all
  positions, so this is a new rule nobody specified. It is tested.
  - `weightedRating` is now exported from `ratings.ts`, and through the barrel
  it becomes public package API.
  - There are extra exports nobody asked for: `isSameSlot`, `familiarityAt`,
  `POSITIONAL_RATING_MIN` and `POSITIONAL_RATING_MAX`.

  **(c) Looks implemented, but weak or questionable**
  - F/S rule ("`F` when Left, Right, Free Role or AM is also 15 or above"): no
  test depends on Right alone. `AM/F RC` also has AM ≥ 15, so dropping
  `has(sides.R)` from `forwardIsF` would still pass. Left (`F LC`), AM and Free
  Role are each pinned by some test.
  - The slot comment says GK and SW have one table each "since CM only ever
  places them centrally". But `SLOTS` contains `SW L` and `SW R`, and the ticket
  needs them to reach 31 cells. So the comment contradicts the data.
  - The `Slot` type also allows `{GK, LC}`. It displays as `GK` but is not
  `isSameSlot` with `GOALKEEPER_SLOT`. The ticket says "invalid inputs are
  unrepresentable by type", though it says this about ratings.
  - `compactLabel` returns `""` when no line reaches 15, even if sides qualify.
  The research and notes don't cover this case, and no test covers it.
  - The overall-rating test "is the best Position Rating among Natural cells"
  has only one natural table, D-central. It would not catch a regression that
  picks the first natural cell instead of the best.

  **Mutation checks that the tests do catch:** WB applied to central D/DM, WB
  missing on wide DM, AM − 5 changed to another offset, LC/RC reading C, the L/R
  mix-up, the threshold moved from ≥ 15 to > 15, the GK short-circuit (sides
  included), each M/AM suppression branch including the M ≥ 15 exception (via
  `AM/F RC`), and tier edges at 18/17 and 15/14.

  the running app.

  To view it:
  1. Run pnpm --filter @cm-clone/desktop dev.
  2. Open a career and go to Tactics → editor.
  3. Click the yellow Tactics prototype → button, bottom right.
  4. Cycle variants with the yellow bar's arrows or the ← / → keys.

  The prototype only appears in development builds. Without it, the Tactics
  editor is unchanged.

  The four variants:
  - A, CM classic: one dense screen laid out like CM 03/04. Quick Load and Save
    As along the top, the pitch grid on the left, the selected player's
    instructions on the right, then team instructions, set pieces and priorities
    underneath.
  - B, Tabbed workspace: tabs for Shape, Team, Players, Set pieces and Library.
    Every instruction is a row of buttons, so all its values are visible, and
    the library groups the 29 presets by back line, plus your saved tactics.
  - C, Slot spreadsheet: the eleven slots are rows and every instruction is a
    column, editable in place, so you compare the whole side at a glance.
    There's a small pitch alongside, and set pieces fold away.
  - D, Live match: only the levers you'd pull mid-match (a mentality stepper, a
    shape swap, and Men Behind The Ball, Counter Attack and Offside Trap
    switches). Changes queue with "Takes effect at 64′", matching the
    live-change rule we decided.

  Common to all:
  - The pitch has all 31 cells, with run arrows drawn as dashed lines.
  - Each player marker shows its fit (Natural, Competent or Unfamiliar) as a
    word, not just a colour.
  - In A, B and C, clicking a player then an empty cell moves him.
  - Loading a preset keeps each player in his slot number.

  How it's stored: it's committed only on the throwaway branch
  prototype/tactics-screen, which I created without switching branches, so the
  other session wasn't disturbed. On dev the files sit uncommitted: the
  prototype/ folders plus a two-line router change. Nothing prototype-related is
  in dev's history.

  Which variant do you want, or which parts from each (say, "A's layout with B's
  library")? I'll record the verdict on ticket 12, remove the prototype files
  from dev, and write the formations spec.

⏺ You picked A. The two CM screenshots settle a lot about its structure:
  - Layout: a Team Selection list on the left, and a right panel that switches
    between Set Positions (the pitch), Set Instructions, and Set Priorities.
  - The list: starters in slot order, then bench, then reserves. Columns are
    shirt number, player, Pos (the compact label) and rating. The captain has a
    "Capt" badge.
  - Settings: each instruction is a tick box plus a dropdown. Unticked, the
    dropdown is greyed and reads "Team (Mixed)". The "often" flags show a hint
    line when ticked, "Set To Preset" sits at the bottom, and a Set Piece
    Instructions block comes below.
  - The pitch: vertical and striped, with numbered circles, "Surname, F" labels
    and dotted runs.

⏺ I've rebuilt variant A to match your two screenshots. Open it as before:
  Tactics → editor → Tactics prototype →; variant A is the default. It
  typechecks, lints clean, and a quick test drove every mode; I haven't looked
  at it in the running app.

  What changed:
  - Menu bar: File ▾ (Quick Load of any of the 29 presets or a saved tactic,
    Save, Save As…) and View ▾ (show or hide the Pos, Fit and Condition columns,
    Pick best eleven). Set Positions, Set Instructions ▾ and Set Priorities ▾
    sit on the right.
  - Team Selection list, always on the left: shirt-number chips (green for
    starters, blue for substitutes, dimmed reserves), the Capt badge, the Pos
    column with the compact label, the slot and its fit, and condition. Click a
    starter to select him, then click a substitute to swap him in.
  - Set Positions: a vertical striped pitch with numbered red circles (the
    keeper in green), "Surname, F" labels and dotted run lines. Clicking a faint
    spot moves the selected player there, and an unfamiliar fit gets a dashed
    orange ring.
  - Set Instructions ▸ Player: "Instructions for Bruce (M L)" with CM's
    tick-box-plus-dropdown rows:
    - Unticked rows show "Team (Mixed)" or "(default)" greyed out.
    - Ticked "often" flags show CM's yellow hint, such as "(Player will attempt
      more crosses)".
    - Set To Preset sits at the bottom, and Distribution only appears for the
      keeper.
    - Below it is "Set Piece Instructions for …" with all six roles and CM's
      exact values.
  - Set Instructions ▸ Team: the nine team instructions in the same row style,
    with their "(mixed)", "(normal)" or "(default)" placeholders, the four
    switches with CM's hint labels, and Team Set Piece Instructions per side.
  - Set Priorities ▸ one of five panels: Captains, Penalty Takers, Free Kick,
    Corner and Throw In Takers, each as ordered lists. CM filled these by drag
    and drop; here you add the selected player and reorder with ▲ ▼.

  This revision is committed on prototype/tactics-screen (fb5a1e1b). dev still
  has the prototype files only as uncommitted changes.

  verdict, remove the prototype files from dev, and write the formations spec.

       5 +## Problem                                                     
       6 +

         +yers](../architecture/2026-09-29-tactic-templates-and-grid-cell
         +-slots.md))                                                    
       8 +carries far more than the v1 screen shows: a 31-cell grid with 
         +runs, a template library, nine team

         +ce roles per slot, team set-piece                              
      10 +instructions per side, and eight ordered taker lists. The scree
         +n could present that as tabs, a                                
      11 +spreadsheet of slots, or CM 03/04's own tactics screen. A proto
         +type built all four                                            
      12 +(`prototype/tactics-screen`, variants A to D) against CM 03/04

      13 +                                                               
      14 +## Proposal                                                    
      15 +

         + variant A):                                                   
      17 +                                                               
      18 +- **Menu bar.** File (quick load any built-in or saved Tactic T
         +emplate, Save, Save As) and View                               
      19 +  (which Team Selection columns show) on the left; Set Position
         +s, Set Instructions (Player, Team) and                         
      20 +  Set Priorities (Captains, Penalty, Free Kick, Corner and Thro
         +w In Takers) on the right. The name of                         
      21 +  the template the Tactic came from shows, with "(modified)" an
         +d the row-count shape beside it.                               
      22 +- **Team Selection list, always on the left.** Starters in slot
         + order, then substitutes, then                                 
      23 +  reserves: shirt number, name, a Capt badge, the compact posit
         +ion label, the slot and its fit tier,                          
      24 +  Condition. Selecting a starter selects his slot; choosing a s

⏺ Ticket 12 is resolved (1edc1aa7), so the formations map is complete: all 19
  tickets have answers. Its spec comes next, and as with the positional spec,
  the skill asks me to agree test seams with you first. Four are needed here,
  because this work spans the engine as well as the screens:

  ❓ Q1 - Seams for the formations spec:
  1. The tactics rules in the shared package (pure functions):
     - The Tactic and Tactic Template types and their validation.
     - The 29 presets, row-count labels and "modified".
     - Every instruction's values and defaults, including the transcribed CM
       templates.
     - Resolving a Tactic into team modifiers and per-slot behaviour.
     - The AI's pre-match choice and in-match rule table.

     Prior art: the shared rules tests.
  2. The match engine:
     - Each setting gets a directional test over many seeded matches, e.g.
       Tackling hard raises fouls and cards, Long Shots often raises long-shot
       chances.
     - Determinism stays tested.
     - A calibration harness checks league averages: goals 2.5–2.8, yellow cards
       3–4, fouls 20–26, and a quarter to a third of goals from set pieces.

     Prior art: the engine's seeded match tests.
  3. The main process on a seeded save:
     - ChangeTactics as full replacement with revision and idempotency.
     - Library create, rename, duplicate, delete and quick load.
     - Live changes at M+1.
     - Set-piece taker fallbacks and the captain handover.
     - AI tactical changes journaled for the human's opponent.
     - Old saves refused.

     Prior art: the main club and match tests, including the seeded-match
     helper.
  4. The Tactics screen: renderer tests for the approved CM layout, covering
     each mode, the tick-box rows, swaps, and keyboard reach for what CM did by
     dragging. Prior art: the existing tactics renderer tests.

  ➡️ Accept these four. The calibration harness also runs as its own script,
  because it simulates too many matches to sit comfortably in the unit suite.

⏺ The formations spec is committed (b4ae7850) and ready for agents. Next, the
  to-tickets step: splitting it into vertical slices for your approval.
  Numbering continues after the 19 decision tickets:

  Proposed breakdown

  20. The tactics rules in the shared package. Blocked by: none.
      - Pure functions: Tactic and Tactic Template types and validation, the 29
        presets, the row-count label and "modified".
      - Every instruction and set-piece value set with its defaults, the
        transcribed CM templates and the seeding rule.
  21. The Tactic is stored and saved as CM's complete Tactic. Blocked by: 20.
      - Storage, contracts and ChangeTactics (full replacement) carry cells,
        runs, the nine team instructions, per-slot instructions and set-piece
        roles, team set pieces, takers and captain.
      - Roles, Tempo, Pressing and Tactical Styles are removed; the preferred
        formation widens to the 29.
      - The current Tactics screen and overview are adapted minimally, showing
        cells, so the app keeps working.
      - A temporary adapter feeds the existing engine from Mentality only.
      - Old saves are refused.
  22. Tactics screen, CM layout: Team Selection and Set Positions. Blocked
      by: 21. The menu bar, the Team Selection list, the pitch with moves, runs
      and substitute swaps, and keyboard equivalents.
  23. Tactics screen: Set Instructions (player and team, tick-box rows, Set To
      Preset, set-piece roles). Blocked by: 22.
  24. Tactics screen: Set Priorities (ordered taker lists and captain, keyboard
      reordering). Blocked by: 22.
  25. The Tactic library. Blocked by: 21. Save, rename, overwrite, duplicate,
      delete and quick load, from main to the File menu.
  26. Engine framework. Blocked by: 21.
      - Resolution to behaviour vectors, the chance pipeline, coverage-scaled
        phases, and the Foul and Offside events.
      - Replaces the temporary adapter. Directional tests for Mentality and
        Passing, plus the calibration script.
  27. Engine: the remaining team-instruction effects (ticket 15's table).
      Blocked by: 26.
  28. Engine: player-instruction effects (ticket 16's table, including
      match-time specific marking). Blocked by: 26.
  29. Engine: set pieces (corners, free kicks, penalties, throw-ins, taker
      fallback, captain handover). Blocked by: 26.
  30. Engine: runs and the out-of-position cost, plus match stats and commentary
      for the new events. Blocked by: 26.
  31. Live tactical changes. Blocked by: 23, 26. The complete Tactic changes
      mid-match at M+1, on the same screen with Confirm, Undo Last and Cancel.
  32. AI: seeded preferences and pre-match tactic choice. Blocked by: 26.
  33. AI: the in-match controller and rule table, journaled for the human's
      opponent. Blocked by: 31, 32.
  34. Contract and docs. Blocked by: everything above, plus positional
      ticket 19.
      - Deletes the Position type and the remaining temporary pieces.
      - Rewrites the glossary, marks the superseded notes, and promotes this
        effort's notes.

  ❓ Q1 - Does the granularity feel right?

  ➡️ Yes. 21 is the widest ticket, but the temporary adapter and the minimal
  screen changes keep it shippable in one step.

  ---

  ❓ Q2 - Are the blocking edges right? After 21, three tracks run in parallel:
  the screen (22–24), the library (25) and the engine (26–30).

  ➡️ Yes.

  ---

  ❓ Q3 - Anything to merge or split?

  ➡️ No changes.
